        query forge_cli_getApplicationDeploymentStatus($appId: ID!, $environmentKey: String!, $id: ID!) {
          appDeployment(appId: $appId, environmentKey: $environmentKey, id: $id) {
            status
            errorDetails {
              code
              message

              description

                __typename

                  newStatus
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }

Query: 
        query forge_cli_getApplicationDeploymentStatus($appId: ID!, $environmentKey: String!, $id: ID!) {
          appDeployment(appId: $appId, environmentKey: $environmentKey, id: $id) {
            status
            errorDetails {
              code
              message
              fields
            }
            stages {
              description
              events {
                __typename
                stepName
                createdAt
                ...on AppDeploymentLogEvent {
                  message
                  level
                }
                ... on AppDeploymentTransitionEvent {
                  newStatus
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }

        query forge_cli_getApplicationDeploymentStatus($appId: ID!, $environmentKey: String!, $id: ID!) {
          appDeployment(appId: $appId, environmentKey: $environmentKey, id: $id) {
            status
            errorDetails {
              code
              message
              fields

              description
              events {
                __typename
                stepName
                createdAt
                ...on AppDeploymentLogEvent {
                  message
                  level
                }
                ... on AppDeploymentTransitionEvent {
                  newStatus
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }

        query forge_cli_getApplicationDeploymentStatus($appId: ID!, $environmentKey: String!, $id: ID!) {
          appDeployment(appId: $appId, environmentKey: $environmentKey, id: $id) {
            status
            errorDetails {
              code
              message
              fields

              description

                __typename
                stepName
                createdAt
                ...on AppDeploymentLogEvent {
                  message
                  level

                  newStatus
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }

        query forge_cli_getApplicationDeploymentStatus($appId: ID!, $environmentKey: String!, $id: ID!) {
          appDeployment(appId: $appId, environmentKey: $environmentKey, id: $id) {
            status
            errorDetails {
              code
              message
              fields
            }
            stages {
              description
              events {
                __typename
                stepName
                createdAt
                ...on AppDeploymentLogEvent {
                  message
                  level
                }
                ... on AppDeploymentTransitionEvent {
                  newStatus
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }

        query forge_cli_getApplicationDeploymentStatus($appId: ID!, $environmentKey: String!, $id: ID!) {
          appDeployment(appId: $appId, environmentKey: $environmentKey, id: $id) {
            status
            errorDetails {
              code
              message
              fields
            }
            stages {
              description
              events {
                __typename
                stepName
                createdAt
                ...on AppDeploymentLogEvent {
                  message
                  level
                }
                ... on AppDeploymentTransitionEvent {
                  newStatus
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }

        query forge_cli_getApplicationDeploymentStatus($appId: ID!, $environmentKey: String!, $id: ID!) {
          appDeployment(appId: $appId, environmentKey: $environmentKey, id: $id) {

                  newStatus

        query forge_cli_getApplicationDeploymentStatus($appId: ID!, $environmentKey: String!, $id: ID!) {
          appDeployment(appId: $appId, environmentKey: $environmentKey, id: $id) {
            status

              code

                  newStatus
                }

            }
          }
        }

        query forge_cli_getApplicationDeploymentStatus($appId: ID!, $environmentKey: String!, $id: ID!) {
          appDeployment(appId: $appId, environmentKey: $environmentKey, id: $id) {
            status

              code

                  newStatus
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }

        query forge_cli_getApplicationDeploymentStatus($appId: ID!, $environmentKey: String!, $id: ID!) {
          appDeployment(appId: $appId, environmentKey: $environmentKey, id: $id) {
            status
            errorDetails {
              code
              message
              fields

              description

                __typename
                stepName
                createdAt

                  message
                  level

                  newStatus
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }

        query forge_cli_getApplicationDeploymentStatus($appId: ID!, $environmentKey: String!, $id: ID!) {
          appDeployment(appId: $appId, environmentKey: $environmentKey, id: $id) {
            status
            errorDetails {
              code
              message
              fields
            }
            stages {
              description
              events {
                __typename
                stepName
                createdAt
                ...on AppDeploymentLogEvent {
                  message
                  level
                }
                ... on AppDeploymentTransitionEvent {
                  newStatus
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }

Query: 
        query forge_cli_getApplicationDeploymentStatus($appId: ID!, $environmentKey: String!, $id: ID!) {
          appDeployment(appId: $appId, environmentKey: $environmentKey, id: $id) {
            status
            errorDetails {
              code
              message
              fields
            }
            stages {
              description
              events {
                __typename
                stepName
                createdAt
                ...on AppDeploymentLogEvent {
                  message
                  level
                }
                ... on AppDeploymentTransitionEvent {
                  newStatus
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
